<file path="riskquant\src\riskquant\config\static_config\validate.py">
```python
# src/riskquant/config/static_config/validate.py
"""
Vectorized validation for the god-tier static config DataFrames.

The new 7-table schema makes most of the legacy "if dim==0 then this
column must be null" checks unnecessary: every table's columns are
required by definition of the table. This validator only needs to
enforce:

  Tier 1 — Required sheets present.
  Tier 2 — Uniqueness (single + composite primary keys).
  Tier 3 — Enum membership for every typed string column.
  Tier 4 — Foreign-key referential integrity across tables.
  Tier 5 — Bespoke domain invariants:
             * FX node naming convention
             * (XAxisKind, RollMethod) allowed-pair table
             * tenor-token grammar matches parent's x_axis_kind
             * discount-curve default & basis invariants
             * every active currency has at least one discount curve
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd

from riskquant.config.enums.asset_classes import (
    ASSET_SUBCLASS_TO_CLASS,
    VALID_ASSET_CLASSES,
    VALID_ASSET_SUBCLASSES,
    AssetClass,
    AssetSubclass,
)
from riskquant.config.enums.discount_basis import (
    DISCOUNT_BASES_EXCLUDING_SENTINEL,
    FIRM_DEFAULT_DISCOUNT_BASIS,
    VALID_DISCOUNT_BASES,
    DiscountBasis,
)
from riskquant.config.enums.quote_types import (
    VALID_QUOTE_TYPES,
    VALID_UNIT_SCALES,
    QuoteType,
)
from riskquant.config.enums.roll_methods import (
    ALLOWED_X_AXIS_KIND_ROLL_METHOD_PAIRS,
    VALID_ROLL_METHODS,
)
from riskquant.config.enums.x_axis_kinds import (
    VALID_X_AXIS_KINDS,
    X_AXIS_KINDS_REQUIRING_ROLL,
    X_AXIS_TOKEN_REGEX,
    XAxisKind,
)
from riskquant.config.enums.y_axis_kinds import VALID_Y_AXIS_KINDS
from riskquant.config.namespace import GlobalIdNamespace, enforce_id_namespace
from riskquant.exceptions import ConfigurationError
from riskquant.market_data.curves.extrap_methods import EXTRAP_METHOD_NAMES
from riskquant.market_data.curves.interp_methods import INTERP_METHOD_NAMES
from riskquant.market_data.risk_factors.shift_types import SHIFT_TYPE_NAMES

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Constants
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

REQUIRED_SHEETS: frozenset[str] = frozenset(
    {
        "ccy",
        "holiday_calendars",
        "spot_roll_rule",
        "node_meta",
        "curve_meta",
        "curve_point_meta",
        "surface_meta",
        "surface_point_meta",
    }
)


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Generic helpers
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------


def _check_required_sheets(dict_of_dfs: dict[str, pd.DataFrame]) -> None:
    missing = REQUIRED_SHEETS - dict_of_dfs.keys()
    if missing:
        raise ConfigurationError(
            f"Missing required sheet(s): {sorted(missing)}.\n"
            f"Required: {sorted(REQUIRED_SHEETS)}"
        )


def _check_columns(df: pd.DataFrame, cols: frozenset[str], sheet_name: str) -> None:
    missing = cols - set(df.columns)
    if missing:
        raise ConfigurationError(
            f"Sheet {sheet_name!r} missing required columns: {sorted(missing)}.\n"
            f"Found: {sorted(df.columns)}"
        )


def _check_unique(df: pd.DataFrame, col: str, sheet_name: str) -> None:
    if not df[col].is_unique:
        dupes = df[col][df[col].duplicated()].unique().tolist()
        raise ConfigurationError(
            f"Uniqueness violation: {sheet_name}.{col} must be unique. "
            f"Duplicates: {dupes}"
        )


def _check_composite_unique(
    df: pd.DataFrame,
    cols: tuple[str, ...],
    sheet_name: str,
) -> None:
    dupes_mask = df.duplicated(subset=list(cols), keep=False)
    if dupes_mask.any():
        offending = df.loc[dupes_mask, list(cols)]
        raise ConfigurationError(
            f"Composite uniqueness violation: "
            f"{sheet_name}({', '.join(cols)}) must be unique.\n"
            f"Duplicates:\n{offending.to_string(index=False)}"
        )


def _check_enum_col(
    df: pd.DataFrame,
    col: str,
    valid_set: frozenset[str],
    sheet_name: str,
    enum_name: str,
    *,
    nullable: bool,
) -> None:
    if col not in df.columns:
        return
    series = df[col]
    populated = series.dropna()
    if not nullable and len(populated) != len(series):
        n_null = int(series.isna().sum())
        raise ConfigurationError(
            f"Enum column {sheet_name}.{col} ({enum_name}) "
            f"contains {n_null} null value(s) but is non-nullable."
        )
    if populated.empty:
        return
    invalid = set(populated.astype(str)) - valid_set
    if invalid:
        raise ConfigurationError(
            f"Enum violation in {sheet_name}.{col}: values not in {enum_name}: "
            f"{sorted(invalid)}.\nValid: {sorted(valid_set)}"
        )


def _check_fk(
    child_df: pd.DataFrame,
    child_col: str,
    parent_df: pd.DataFrame,
    parent_col: str,
    *,
    child_sheet: str,
    parent_sheet: str,
) -> None:
    child_vals = child_df[child_col].dropna().to_numpy()
    parent_vals = parent_df[parent_col].dropna().to_numpy()
    missing = np.setdiff1d(child_vals, parent_vals, assume_unique=False)
    if missing.size > 0:
        raise ConfigurationError(
            f"FK violation: {child_sheet}.{child_col} references values absent "
            f"from {parent_sheet}.{parent_col}.\nMissing: {missing.tolist()[:25]}"
        )


def _check_no_blank_strings(
    df: pd.DataFrame, col: str, sheet_name: str, *, nullable: bool
) -> None:
    if col not in df.columns:
        return
    series = df[col]
    if nullable:
        series = series.dropna()
    if series.empty:
        return
    is_blank = series.astype(str).str.strip() == ""
    if is_blank.any():
        idx = series.index[is_blank].tolist()[:10]
        raise ConfigurationError(
            f"Blank string violation: {sheet_name}.{col} has empty values at "
            f"rows: {idx}"
        )


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Tier 2 — per-sheet uniqueness
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------


def _validate_uniqueness(dict_of_dfs: dict[str, pd.DataFrame]) -> None:
    single_pk: dict[str, tuple[str, ...]] = {
        "ccy": ("ccy_name", "ccy_id"),
        "spot_roll_rule": ("rule_name",),
        "node_meta": ("node_id", "node_name"),
        "curve_meta": ("curve_id", "curve_name"),
        "curve_point_meta": ("rf_id",),
        "surface_meta": ("surface_id", "surface_name"),
        "surface_point_meta": ("rf_id",),
    }
    for sheet_name, cols in single_pk.items():
        df = dict_of_dfs[sheet_name]
        for col in cols:
            _check_unique(df, col, sheet_name)

    _check_composite_unique(
        dict_of_dfs["curve_point_meta"],
        ("curve_id", "tenor_token"),
        "curve_point_meta",
    )
    _check_composite_unique(
        dict_of_dfs["surface_point_meta"],
        ("surface_id", "x_tenor_token", "y_label"),
        "surface_point_meta",
    )


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Tier 3 — enum membership
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------


def _validate_enums(dict_of_dfs: dict[str, pd.DataFrame]) -> None:
    interp_names = frozenset(INTERP_METHOD_NAMES)
    extrap_names = frozenset(EXTRAP_METHOD_NAMES)
    shift_names = frozenset(SHIFT_TYPE_NAMES)

    # --- node_meta ---
    node_df = dict_of_dfs["node_meta"]
    _check_enum_col(
        node_df,
        "asset_class",
        VALID_ASSET_CLASSES,
        "node_meta",
        "AssetClass",
        nullable=False,
    )
    _check_enum_col(
        node_df,
        "asset_subclass",
        VALID_ASSET_SUBCLASSES,
        "node_meta",
        "AssetSubclass",
        nullable=False,
    )
    _check_enum_col(
        node_df,
        "quote_type",
        VALID_QUOTE_TYPES,
        "node_meta",
        "UnitType",
        nullable=False,
    )
    _check_enum_col(
        node_df,
        "unit_scale",
        VALID_UNIT_SCALES,
        "node_meta",
        "UnitScale",
        nullable=False,
    )
    _check_enum_col(
        node_df, "shift_type", shift_names, "node_meta", "ShiftType", nullable=False
    )

    # --- curve_meta ---
    curve_df = dict_of_dfs["curve_meta"]
    _check_enum_col(
        curve_df,
        "asset_class",
        VALID_ASSET_CLASSES,
        "curve_meta",
        "AssetClass",
        nullable=False,
    )
    _check_enum_col(
        curve_df,
        "asset_subclass",
        VALID_ASSET_SUBCLASSES,
        "curve_meta",
        "AssetSubclass",
        nullable=False,
    )
    _check_enum_col(
        curve_df,
        "quote_type",
        VALID_QUOTE_TYPES,
        "curve_meta",
        "UnitType",
        nullable=False,
    )
    _check_enum_col(
        curve_df,
        "unit_scale",
        VALID_UNIT_SCALES,
        "curve_meta",
        "UnitScale",
        nullable=False,
    )
    _check_enum_col(
        curve_df, "shift_type", shift_names, "curve_meta", "ShiftType", nullable=False
    )
    _check_enum_col(
        curve_df,
        "x_axis_kind",
        VALID_X_AXIS_KINDS,
        "curve_meta",
        "XAxisKind",
        nullable=False,
    )
    _check_enum_col(
        curve_df,
        "x_interp_method",
        interp_names,
        "curve_meta",
        "InterpMethod",
        nullable=False,
    )
    _check_enum_col(
        curve_df,
        "x_extrap_method",
        extrap_names,
        "curve_meta",
        "ExtrapMethod",
        nullable=False,
    )
    _check_enum_col(
        curve_df,
        "discount_basis",
        VALID_DISCOUNT_BASES,
        "curve_meta",
        "DiscountBasis",
        nullable=False,
    )

    # --- surface_meta ---
    surf_df = dict_of_dfs["surface_meta"]
    _check_enum_col(
        surf_df,
        "asset_class",
        VALID_ASSET_CLASSES,
        "surface_meta",
        "AssetClass",
        nullable=False,
    )
    _check_enum_col(
        surf_df,
        "asset_subclass",
        VALID_ASSET_SUBCLASSES,
        "surface_meta",
        "AssetSubclass",
        nullable=False,
    )
    _check_enum_col(
        surf_df,
        "quote_type",
        VALID_QUOTE_TYPES,
        "surface_meta",
        "UnitType",
        nullable=False,
    )
    _check_enum_col(
        surf_df,
        "unit_scale",
        VALID_UNIT_SCALES,
        "surface_meta",
        "UnitScale",
        nullable=False,
    )
    _check_enum_col(
        surf_df, "shift_type", shift_names, "surface_meta", "ShiftType", nullable=False
    )
    _check_enum_col(
        surf_df,
        "x_axis_kind",
        VALID_X_AXIS_KINDS,
        "surface_meta",
        "XAxisKind",
        nullable=False,
    )
    _check_enum_col(
        surf_df,
        "x_interp_method",
        interp_names,
        "surface_meta",
        "InterpMethod",
        nullable=False,
    )
    _check_enum_col(
        surf_df,
        "x_extrap_method",
        extrap_names,
        "surface_meta",
        "ExtrapMethod",
        nullable=False,
    )
    _check_enum_col(
        surf_df,
        "y_axis_kind",
        VALID_Y_AXIS_KINDS,
        "surface_meta",
        "YAxisKind",
        nullable=False,
    )
    _check_enum_col(
        surf_df,
        "y_interp_method",
        interp_names,
        "surface_meta",
        "InterpMethod",
        nullable=False,
    )
    _check_enum_col(
        surf_df,
        "y_extrap_method",
        extrap_names,
        "surface_meta",
        "ExtrapMethod",
        nullable=False,
    )

    # --- spot_roll_rule ---
    srr_df = dict_of_dfs["spot_roll_rule"]
    _check_enum_col(
        srr_df,
        "roll_method",
        VALID_ROLL_METHODS,
        "spot_roll_rule",
        "RollMethod",
        nullable=False,
    )


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Tier 4 — foreign keys
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------


def _validate_fks(dict_of_dfs: dict[str, pd.DataFrame]) -> None:
    ccy_df = dict_of_dfs["ccy"]
    node_df = dict_of_dfs["node_meta"]
    curve_df = dict_of_dfs["curve_meta"]
    curve_pt_df = dict_of_dfs["curve_point_meta"]
    surf_df = dict_of_dfs["surface_meta"]
    surf_pt_df = dict_of_dfs["surface_point_meta"]
    srr_df = dict_of_dfs["spot_roll_rule"]

    # ccy FKs
    _check_fk(
        node_df,
        "quote_ccy",
        ccy_df,
        "ccy_name",
        child_sheet="node_meta",
        parent_sheet="ccy",
    )
    _check_fk(
        curve_df,
        "quote_ccy",
        ccy_df,
        "ccy_name",
        child_sheet="curve_meta",
        parent_sheet="ccy",
    )
    _check_fk(
        surf_df,
        "quote_ccy",
        ccy_df,
        "ccy_name",
        child_sheet="surface_meta",
        parent_sheet="ccy",
    )

    # node_meta.fx_base_ccy is nullable; FK only on populated rows
    populated_fx_base = node_df.loc[node_df["fx_base_ccy"].notna(), ["fx_base_ccy"]]
    if not populated_fx_base.empty:
        _check_fk(
            populated_fx_base,
            "fx_base_ccy",
            ccy_df,
            "ccy_name",
            child_sheet="node_meta",
            parent_sheet="ccy",
        )

    # Curve / surface spot_roll_rule_name FKs (only populated rows)
    cm_pop = curve_df.loc[
        curve_df["spot_roll_rule_name"].notna(), ["spot_roll_rule_name"]
    ]
    if not cm_pop.empty:
        _check_fk(
            cm_pop,
            "spot_roll_rule_name",
            srr_df,
            "rule_name",
            child_sheet="curve_meta",
            parent_sheet="spot_roll_rule",
        )

    sm_pop = surf_df.loc[
        surf_df["spot_roll_rule_name"].notna(), ["spot_roll_rule_name"]
    ]
    if not sm_pop.empty:
        _check_fk(
            sm_pop,
            "spot_roll_rule_name",
            srr_df,
            "rule_name",
            child_sheet="surface_meta",
            parent_sheet="spot_roll_rule",
        )

    # Curve / surface point parent FKs
    _check_fk(
        curve_pt_df,
        "curve_id",
        curve_df,
        "curve_id",
        child_sheet="curve_point_meta",
        parent_sheet="curve_meta",
    )
    _check_fk(
        surf_pt_df,
        "surface_id",
        surf_df,
        "surface_id",
        child_sheet="surface_point_meta",
        parent_sheet="surface_meta",
    )


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Tier 5 — bespoke domain invariants
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------


def _validate_global_name_uniqueness(dict_of_dfs: dict[str, pd.DataFrame]) -> None:
    """No name appears in more than one of {node, curve, surface} namespaces."""
    node_names = set(dict_of_dfs["node_meta"]["node_name"].astype(str))
    curve_names = set(dict_of_dfs["curve_meta"]["curve_name"].astype(str))
    surface_names = set(dict_of_dfs["surface_meta"]["surface_name"].astype(str))

    nc = node_names & curve_names
    ns = node_names & surface_names
    cs = curve_names & surface_names

    overlap = nc | ns | cs
    if overlap:
        raise ConfigurationError(
            f"Name collision across container namespaces: {sorted(overlap)}.\n"
            f"Every node_name / curve_name / surface_name must be globally unique."
        )


def _validate_rf_id_uniqueness(dict_of_dfs: dict[str, pd.DataFrame]) -> None:
    """rf_ids (node_id ∪ curve_point.rf_id ∪ surface_point.rf_id) must be globally unique."""
    node_ids = dict_of_dfs["node_meta"]["node_id"].to_numpy()
    cp_ids = dict_of_dfs["curve_point_meta"]["rf_id"].to_numpy()
    sp_ids = dict_of_dfs["surface_point_meta"]["rf_id"].to_numpy()

    all_rf_ids = np.concatenate([node_ids, cp_ids, sp_ids])

    if np.unique(all_rf_ids).size != all_rf_ids.size:
        unique, counts = np.unique(all_rf_ids, return_counts=True)
        dupes = unique[counts > 1].tolist()
        raise ConfigurationError(
            f"CRITICAL: rf_id collision across atomic namespaces: {dupes[:25]}.\n"
            f"node_id, curve_point.rf_id, and surface_point.rf_id share one ID space "
            f"and cannot overlap."
        )


def _validate_container_id_uniqueness(dict_of_dfs: dict[str, pd.DataFrame]) -> None:
    """Ensure internal primary key uniqueness within container tables."""
    # Cross-container collisions are prevented by the namespace bounds.
    # We only need to check that the tables themselves don't have duplicate IDs.
    for table, col in [("curve_meta", "curve_id"), ("surface_meta", "surface_id")]:
        ids = dict_of_dfs[table][col].to_numpy()
        if np.unique(ids).size != ids.size:
            raise ConfigurationError(
                f"CRITICAL: Duplicate primary keys found in {table}.{col}."
            )


def _validate_global_id_namespace(dict_of_dfs: dict[str, pd.DataFrame]) -> None:
    """IDs that share a namespace must be globally bounded."""
    # 1. Gather ALL atomic Risk Factor IDs (Dimension = 0)
    node_ids = dict_of_dfs["node_meta"][
        "node_id"
    ].to_numpy()  # Node IDs are RF IDs by definition
    cp_ids = dict_of_dfs["curve_point_meta"]["rf_id"].to_numpy()
    sp_ids = dict_of_dfs["surface_point_meta"]["rf_id"].to_numpy()
    all_rf_ids = np.concatenate([node_ids, cp_ids, sp_ids])

    # 2. Gather Containers
    curve_ids = dict_of_dfs["curve_meta"]["curve_id"].to_numpy()
    surface_ids = dict_of_dfs["surface_meta"]["surface_id"].to_numpy()

    # 3. Enforce the mathematical bounds
    enforce_id_namespace(ids=all_rf_ids, bounds=GlobalIdNamespace.NODES)
    enforce_id_namespace(ids=curve_ids, bounds=GlobalIdNamespace.CURVES)
    enforce_id_namespace(ids=surface_ids, bounds=GlobalIdNamespace.SURFACES)


def _validate_asset_class_subclass_consistency(
    dict_of_dfs: dict[str, pd.DataFrame],
) -> None:
    """asset_subclass must map to the declared asset_class for all 3 meta tables."""

    def _expected_class(subclass_val: str) -> str | None:
        try:
            return ASSET_SUBCLASS_TO_CLASS[AssetSubclass(subclass_val)].value
        except (ValueError, KeyError):
            # AssetSubclass(...) raises ValueError on an unknown value;
            # the dict lookup raises KeyError if a member is unmapped.
            # (Unreachable once _validate_enums has run, but kept honest.)
            return None

    for sheet in ("node_meta", "curve_meta", "surface_meta"):
        df = dict_of_dfs[sheet]
        if "asset_class" not in df.columns or "asset_subclass" not in df.columns:
            continue
        valid = df[["asset_class", "asset_subclass"]].dropna()
        if valid.empty:
            continue
        expected = valid["asset_subclass"].map(_expected_class)
        bad_mask = expected.notna() & (valid["asset_class"] != expected)
        if bad_mask.any():
            offending = valid.loc[bad_mask].copy()
            offending["expected_class"] = expected[bad_mask]
            raise ConfigurationError(
                f"Domain violation: {sheet} has inconsistent "
                f"asset_class / asset_subclass.\n"
                f"{offending.to_string(index=False)}"
            )


def _validate_fx_node_invariants(dict_of_dfs: dict[str, pd.DataFrame]) -> None:
    """
    FX rate node contract:

    1. unit_type == FX_RATE  =>  fx_base_ccy must be populated.
    2. unit_type != FX_RATE  =>  fx_base_ccy must be null.
    3. FX_RATE node_name must equal fx_base_ccy + quote_ccy.
    4. FX_RATE pairs (base, quote) must be unique.
    """
    df = dict_of_dfs["node_meta"]

    is_fx_rate = df["quote_type"] == QuoteType.FX_RATE.value

    missing_base = is_fx_rate & df["fx_base_ccy"].isna()
    if missing_base.any():
        offending = df.loc[
            missing_base,
            [
                "node_name",
                "quote_type",
                "fx_base_ccy",
                "quote_ccy",
            ],
        ]
        raise ConfigurationError(
            "Domain violation: FX_RATE nodes must specify fx_base_ccy.\n"
            f"{offending.to_string(index=False)}"
        )

    unexpected_base = ~is_fx_rate & df["fx_base_ccy"].notna()
    if unexpected_base.any():
        offending = df.loc[
            unexpected_base,
            [
                "node_name",
                "asset_class",
                "quote_type",
                "fx_base_ccy",
            ],
        ]
        raise ConfigurationError(
            "Domain violation: only FX_RATE nodes may specify fx_base_ccy.\n"
            f"{offending.to_string(index=False)}"
        )

    fx_rate_rows = df.loc[is_fx_rate]

    if fx_rate_rows.empty:
        return

    expected_names = fx_rate_rows["fx_base_ccy"].astype(str) + fx_rate_rows[
        "quote_ccy"
    ].astype(str)

    name_mismatch = fx_rate_rows["node_name"] != expected_names

    if name_mismatch.any():
        offending = fx_rate_rows.loc[
            name_mismatch,
            ["node_name", "fx_base_ccy", "quote_ccy"],
        ]

        raise ConfigurationError(
            "Domain violation: FX_RATE node_name must equal "
            "fx_base_ccy + quote_ccy.\n"
            f"{offending.to_string(index=False)}"
        )

    from collections import Counter

    pairs = list(
        zip(
            fx_rate_rows["fx_base_ccy"],
            fx_rate_rows["quote_ccy"],
            strict=True,
        )
    )

    duplicate_pairs = [pair for pair, count in Counter(pairs).items() if count > 1]

    if duplicate_pairs:
        raise ConfigurationError(
            "FX pair duplication: "
            f"(base, quote) pairs appear more than once: "
            f"{duplicate_pairs}"
        )


def _validate_axis_roll_pairs(dict_of_dfs: dict[str, pd.DataFrame]) -> None:
    """
    (XAxisKind, RollMethod) compatibility:

      - LABEL curves must have spot_roll_rule_name == NULL.
      - OFFSET_* curves must have spot_roll_rule_name populated.
      - The resolved (XAxisKind, RollMethod) pair must be in the
        ALLOWED_X_AXIS_KIND_ROLL_METHOD_PAIRS table.

    Vectorized: a single pass over each meta table, no row loops.
    """
    srr_df = dict_of_dfs["spot_roll_rule"]
    rule_to_method: dict[str, str] = dict(
        zip(
            srr_df["rule_name"].astype(str),
            srr_df["roll_method"].astype(str),
            strict=True,
        )
    )

    # Pre-compute the allowed-pair set as plain strings (cheap O(K) build).
    allowed_pair_names: frozenset[tuple[str, str]] = frozenset(
        (xk.name, rm.name) for xk, rm in ALLOWED_X_AXIS_KIND_ROLL_METHOD_PAIRS
    )

    for sheet, name_col in (
        ("curve_meta", "curve_name"),
        ("surface_meta", "surface_name"),
    ):
        df = dict_of_dfs[sheet]
        if df.empty:
            continue

        x_kind = df["x_axis_kind"].astype(str)
        rule = df["spot_roll_rule_name"]
        rule_populated = rule.notna()
        needs_roll = x_kind.isin(X_AXIS_KINDS_REQUIRING_ROLL)

        # Missing rule on OFFSET container.
        missing = needs_roll & ~rule_populated
        if missing.any():
            offending = df.loc[missing, [name_col, "x_axis_kind"]]
            raise ConfigurationError(
                f"{sheet}: OFFSET containers missing spot_roll_rule_name:\n"
                f"{offending.to_string(index=False)}"
            )

        # Populated rule on LABEL container.
        extraneous = ~needs_roll & rule_populated
        if extraneous.any():
            offending = df.loc[
                extraneous, [name_col, "x_axis_kind", "spot_roll_rule_name"]
            ]
            raise ConfigurationError(
                f"{sheet}: LABEL containers with populated spot_roll_rule_name:\n"
                f"{offending.to_string(index=False)}"
            )

        # For OFFSET rows, check the (x_kind, method) pair is allowed.
        offset_rows = df.loc[needs_roll].copy()
        if offset_rows.empty:
            continue
        method = offset_rows["spot_roll_rule_name"].astype(str).map(rule_to_method)
        # Vectorized pair check: encode each (x_kind, roll_method) tuple as a
        # single "X|M" string and isin() against the precomputed allowed set.
        # Drops the Python-level list comprehension in the validation hot path.
        joined = offset_rows["x_axis_kind"].astype(str) + "|" + method.astype(str)
        allowed_join = frozenset(f"{xk}|{rm}" for xk, rm in allowed_pair_names)
        bad_mask = ~joined.isin(allowed_join).to_numpy(copy=False)
        if bad_mask.any():
            offending = offset_rows.loc[
                bad_mask, [name_col, "x_axis_kind", "spot_roll_rule_name"]
            ].copy()
            offending["roll_method"] = method[bad_mask].values
            raise ConfigurationError(
                f"{sheet}: disallowed (x_axis_kind, roll_method) combinations:\n"
                f"{offending.to_string(index=False)}"
            )


def _validate_fh_bh_mmdd(dict_of_dfs: dict[str, pd.DataFrame]) -> None:
    fh_bh_mmdd = dict_of_dfs["spot_roll_rule"]["fh_bh_mmdd"]
    if fh_bh_mmdd.empty:
        return
    fh_bh_mmdd = fh_bh_mmdd.dropna().astype(str)

    # Assert correct length of 9 (e.g., 0401|1001)
    bad_length = fh_bh_mmdd.str.len() != 9
    if bad_length.any():
        offending = fh_bh_mmdd[bad_length]
        raise ConfigurationError(
            f"Invalid fh_bh_mmdd length:\n{offending.to_string(index=False)}"
        )

    # Assert correct format of 4 digits, pipe, 4 digits.
    bad_format = ~fh_bh_mmdd.str.match(r"^\d{4}\|\d{4}$")
    if bad_format.any():
        offending = fh_bh_mmdd[bad_format]
        raise ConfigurationError(
            f"Invalid fh_bh_mmdd format (expected 'MMDD|MMDD'):\n"
            f"{offending.to_string(index=False)}"
        )


def _validate_curve_point_token_grammar(
    dict_of_dfs: dict[str, pd.DataFrame],
) -> None:
    """
    Every curve_point.tenor_token matches its parent's x_axis_kind regex.

    Vectorized: group rows by parent x_axis_kind, apply pd.Series.str.match
    with the kind-specific regex to each group in one vectorized call.
    """
    cm_df = dict_of_dfs["curve_meta"]
    cp_df = dict_of_dfs["curve_point_meta"]
    if cp_df.empty:
        return

    # Join parent x_axis_kind onto each point row via map (vectorized).
    parent_kind_by_id = cm_df.set_index("curve_id")["x_axis_kind"].astype(str)
    work = cp_df[["curve_id", "rf_id", "tenor_token"]].copy()
    work["x_axis_kind"] = work["curve_id"].map(parent_kind_by_id)

    bad_pieces: list[pd.DataFrame] = []
    for kind_name, group in work.groupby("x_axis_kind"):
        # Explicitly cast kind_name to a string
        kind_name_str = str(kind_name)
        regex = X_AXIS_TOKEN_REGEX[XAxisKind[kind_name_str]]
        good = group["tenor_token"].astype(str).str.match(regex)
        bad = ~good
        if bad.any():
            bad_pieces.append(group.loc[bad])

    if bad_pieces:
        offending = pd.concat(bad_pieces, ignore_index=True)
        raise ConfigurationError(
            "Curve point token grammar violations:\n"
            f"{offending.head(25).to_string(index=False)}"
        )


def _validate_surface_point_token_grammar(
    dict_of_dfs: dict[str, pd.DataFrame],
) -> None:
    """
    Every surface_point.x_tenor_token matches its parent's x_axis_kind regex.

    Vectorized: same per-kind-group str.match pattern as the curve-point
    validator.
    """
    sm_df = dict_of_dfs["surface_meta"]
    sp_df = dict_of_dfs["surface_point_meta"]
    if sp_df.empty:
        return

    parent_kind_by_id = sm_df.set_index("surface_id")["x_axis_kind"].astype(str)
    work = sp_df[["surface_id", "rf_id", "x_tenor_token"]].copy()
    work["x_axis_kind"] = work["surface_id"].map(parent_kind_by_id)

    bad_pieces: list[pd.DataFrame] = []
    for kind_name, group in work.groupby("x_axis_kind"):
        # Explicitly cast kind_name to a string
        kind_name_str = str(kind_name)
        regex = X_AXIS_TOKEN_REGEX[XAxisKind[kind_name_str]]
        good = group["x_tenor_token"].astype(str).str.match(regex)
        bad = ~good
        if bad.any():
            bad_pieces.append(group.loc[bad])

    if bad_pieces:
        offending = pd.concat(bad_pieces, ignore_index=True)
        raise ConfigurationError(
            "Surface point token grammar violations:\n"
            f"{offending.head(25).to_string(index=False)}"
        )


def _validate_discount_invariants(dict_of_dfs: dict[str, pd.DataFrame]) -> None:
    """
    Discount-curve rules:
      - is_default_discount_for_ccy=True curves must have a real basis
        (not NOT_A_DISCOUNT_CURVE).
      - Non-discount curves (basis=NOT_A_DISCOUNT_CURVE) cannot be default.
      - Discount curves must have asset_class=='IR'.
      - The default's basis must equal FIRM_DEFAULT_DISCOUNT_BASIS.
      - Exactly one default per currency that has any discount curve.
      - (ccy, basis) is unique among discount curves.
      - Every currency in the ccy table that appears as a quote_ccy on
        any other container must have at least one discount curve.
    """
    curve_df = dict_of_dfs["curve_meta"]
    node_df = dict_of_dfs["node_meta"]
    surf_df = dict_of_dfs["surface_meta"]
    # ccy_df = dict_of_dfs["ccy"]

    is_disc = curve_df["discount_basis"] != DiscountBasis.NOT_A_DISCOUNT_CURVE.name
    is_default = curve_df["is_default_discount_for_ccy"].astype(bool)

    # Default => is a real discount curve.
    bad = is_default & ~is_disc
    if bad.any():
        offending = curve_df.loc[bad, ["curve_name", "discount_basis"]]
        raise ConfigurationError(
            "Default discount flag set on non-discount curve(s):\n"
            f"{offending.to_string(index=False)}"
        )

    # Discount curves must be IR.
    bad_class = is_disc & (curve_df["asset_class"] != AssetClass.IR.value)
    if bad_class.any():
        offending = curve_df.loc[bad_class, ["curve_name", "asset_class"]]
        raise ConfigurationError(
            "Discount curves must have asset_class=='IR'.\n"
            f"{offending.to_string(index=False)}"
        )

    # Default basis must equal the firm standard.
    bad_basis = is_default & (
        curve_df["discount_basis"] != FIRM_DEFAULT_DISCOUNT_BASIS.name
    )
    if bad_basis.any():
        offending = curve_df.loc[bad_basis, ["curve_name", "discount_basis"]]
        raise ConfigurationError(
            f"Default discount curves must use the firm-standard basis "
            f"{FIRM_DEFAULT_DISCOUNT_BASIS.name!r}.\n"
            f"{offending.to_string(index=False)}"
        )

    # Exactly one default per currency that has any discount curve.
    disc_curves = curve_df.loc[
        is_disc,
        ["curve_name", "quote_ccy", "discount_basis", "is_default_discount_for_ccy"],
    ]
    default_counts = (
        disc_curves.groupby("quote_ccy")["is_default_discount_for_ccy"]
        .sum()
        .astype(int)
    )
    bad_ccys = default_counts[default_counts != 1]
    if not bad_ccys.empty:
        raise ConfigurationError(
            f"Each currency with a discount curve must flag exactly one as "
            f"default. Currencies with !=1 default:\n"
            f"{bad_ccys.to_string()}"
        )

    # (ccy, basis) is unique among discount curves.
    composite = disc_curves[["quote_ccy", "discount_basis"]]
    dupe_mask = composite.duplicated(keep=False)
    if dupe_mask.any():
        offending = disc_curves.loc[dupe_mask]
        raise ConfigurationError(
            "Duplicate (quote_ccy, discount_basis) among discount curves:\n"
            f"{offending.to_string(index=False)}"
        )

    # Coverage: every currency that's a quote_ccy somewhere must have a
    # discount curve. (Skips currencies that exist in `ccy` but aren't used.)
    quote_ccys_used = pd.Index(
        pd.concat([curve_df["quote_ccy"], surf_df["quote_ccy"], node_df["quote_ccy"]])
        .dropna()
        .unique()
    )
    ccys_with_discount = set(disc_curves["quote_ccy"].astype(str))
    missing = sorted(set(quote_ccys_used.astype(str)) - ccys_with_discount)
    if missing:
        raise ConfigurationError(
            f"Currencies with no discount curve: {missing}. "
            f"Every active quote_ccy must have at least one discount curve."
        )

    # Sanity: discount_basis used by discount curves must be one of the real bases.
    real_bases = set(disc_curves["discount_basis"].astype(str))
    bogus = real_bases - DISCOUNT_BASES_EXCLUDING_SENTINEL
    if bogus:
        raise ConfigurationError(
            f"Unexpected discount_basis on discount curves: {sorted(bogus)}. "
            f"Allowed: {sorted(DISCOUNT_BASES_EXCLUDING_SENTINEL)}"
        )


def _validate_no_blanks(dict_of_dfs: dict[str, pd.DataFrame]) -> None:
    """Hard fail on empty / whitespace-only strings in non-nullable string cols."""
    string_cols_by_sheet: dict[str, dict[str, bool]] = {
        "ccy": {"ccy_name": False},
        "node_meta": {
            "node_name": False,
            "quote_ccy": False,
            "asset_class": False,
            "asset_subclass": False,
            "quote_type": False,
            "unit_scale": False,
            "shift_type": False,
            "fx_base_ccy": True,
        },
        "curve_meta": {
            "curve_name": False,
            "quote_ccy": False,
            "asset_class": False,
            "asset_subclass": False,
            "quote_type": False,
            "unit_scale": False,
            "shift_type": False,
            "x_axis_kind": False,
            "x_interp_method": False,
            "x_extrap_method": False,
            "spot_roll_rule_name": True,
            "discount_basis": False,
        },
        "curve_point_meta": {"tenor_token": False},
        "surface_meta": {
            "surface_name": False,
            "quote_ccy": False,
            "asset_class": False,
            "asset_subclass": False,
            "quote_type": False,
            "unit_scale": False,
            "shift_type": False,
            "x_axis_kind": False,
            "x_interp_method": False,
            "x_extrap_method": False,
            "spot_roll_rule_name": True,
            "y_axis_kind": False,
            "y_interp_method": False,
            "y_extrap_method": False,
        },
        "surface_point_meta": {"x_tenor_token": False, "y_label": False},
        "spot_roll_rule": {
            "rule_name": False,
            "roll_method": False,
            "fh_bh_mmdd": True,
        },
    }
    for sheet_name, cols in string_cols_by_sheet.items():
        df = dict_of_dfs[sheet_name]
        for col, nullable in cols.items():
            _check_no_blank_strings(df, col, sheet_name, nullable=nullable)


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Public entry point
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------


def validate_static_config_dfs(dict_of_dfs: dict[str, pd.DataFrame]) -> None:
    """
    Execute all validation tiers against the loaded config DataFrames.

    Raises ConfigurationError on the first violation with a descriptive message.
    """
    _check_required_sheets(dict_of_dfs)

    expected_columns: dict[str, frozenset[str]] = {
        "ccy": frozenset({"ccy_name", "ccy_id"}),
        "holiday_calendars": frozenset({"holiday_calendar_name", "holiday_date"}),
        "spot_roll_rule": frozenset(
            {
                "rule_name",
                "roll_method",
                "offset_in_tenor_units",
                "roll_month",
                "roll_day",
                "fh_bh_mmdd",
                "post_roll_shift",
            }
        ),
        "node_meta": frozenset(
            {
                "node_id",
                "node_name",
                "quote_ccy",
                "asset_class",
                "asset_subclass",
                "quote_type",
                "unit_scale",
                "shift_type",
                "fx_base_ccy",
            }
        ),
        "curve_meta": frozenset(
            {
                "curve_id",
                "curve_name",
                "quote_ccy",
                "asset_class",
                "asset_subclass",
                "quote_type",
                "unit_scale",
                "shift_type",
                "x_axis_kind",
                "x_interp_method",
                "x_extrap_method",
                "spot_roll_rule_name",
                "discount_basis",
                "is_default_discount_for_ccy",
            }
        ),
        "curve_point_meta": frozenset({"rf_id", "curve_id", "tenor_token"}),
        "surface_meta": frozenset(
            {
                "surface_id",
                "surface_name",
                "quote_ccy",
                "asset_class",
                "asset_subclass",
                "quote_type",
                "unit_scale",
                "shift_type",
                "x_axis_kind",
                "x_interp_method",
                "x_extrap_method",
                "spot_roll_rule_name",
                "y_axis_kind",
                "y_interp_method",
                "y_extrap_method",
            }
        ),
        "surface_point_meta": frozenset(
            {
                "rf_id",
                "surface_id",
                "x_tenor_token",
                "y_label",
            }
        ),
    }
    for sheet_name, cols in expected_columns.items():
        _check_columns(dict_of_dfs[sheet_name], cols, sheet_name)

    _validate_no_blanks(dict_of_dfs)
    _validate_uniqueness(dict_of_dfs)
    _validate_enums(dict_of_dfs)
    _validate_fks(dict_of_dfs)

    # Tier 5 — domain
    _validate_rf_id_uniqueness(dict_of_dfs)
    _validate_container_id_uniqueness(dict_of_dfs)
    _validate_global_id_namespace(dict_of_dfs)
    _validate_asset_class_subclass_consistency(dict_of_dfs)
    _validate_fx_node_invariants(dict_of_dfs)
    _validate_axis_roll_pairs(dict_of_dfs)
    _validate_curve_point_token_grammar(dict_of_dfs)
    _validate_surface_point_token_grammar(dict_of_dfs)
    _validate_discount_invariants(dict_of_dfs)
    _validate_fh_bh_mmdd(dict_of_dfs)
```

```python
# src/riskquant/constants.py

"""
Common constants used across the riskquant codebase.

This module separates:
- typed sentinel values used in numeric payloads / arrays
- general configuration constants used by the portfolio engine
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from datetime import date

import numpy as np

# ==============================================================================
# GLOBAL SENTINELS
# ==============================================================================

# Universal sentinel for "no valid ID".
# Used across instrument_ids, underlying_ids, volatility_ids, and discount_curve_ids.
NO_ID = np.int32(-999_999)

# Universal sentinel for missing dates (settlement, delivery, expiry).
# Using -1 allows for clean `date_ord >= 0` masking.
NO_DATE_ORD32 = np.int32(-99_999)

# Specific sentinel for FX: No conversion required (Underlying CCY == Settlement CCY)
NO_FX_RF_REQUIRED = np.int32(-1)

# Invalid UIX sentinel for any universe index (*_uix fields).
# Used exclusively in the Execution Engine (blocks/compilers), never in Ingest.
INVALID_UIX = np.int32(-999)

# NOT IMPLEMENTED sentinels for fields expected to be populated but aren't yet.
NOT_IMPLEMENTED_SENTINEL: np.int8 = np.int8(-99)

# ==============================================================================
# OPTION ENCODING
# ==============================================================================

# 1 for call options, -1 for put options, 0 for not-an-option.
# Allows for branchless payoff calculation: payoff * cp_mult
CALL_OPTION_MULT = np.int8(1)
PUT_OPTION_MULT = np.int8(-1)
NOT_OPTION_MULT = np.int8(0)

# ==============================================================================
# Date encoding
# ==============================================================================

DATE_EPOCH: date = date(1970, 1, 1)

# ==============================================================================
# CONFIGURATION CONSTANTS
# ==============================================================================

ROUND_DECIMALS_LEVEL = 8
ROUND_DECIMALS_QUANTITY = 8
ROUND_DECIMALS_STRIKE_PRICE = 8

POSITION_DUST_TOLERANCE = 1e-8
PD_DUST_TOLERANCE = 1e-10

# ==============================================================================
# DEVELOPMENT-ONLY FALLBACK VALUES
# ==============================================================================

FALLBACK_MOCK_VOLATILITY_DO_NOT_SHIP: float = 0.50

# ==============================================================================
# EWMA Volatility Calculation Constants
# ==============================================================================

EWMA_DECAY_FACTOR: float = 0.94
MAX_EWMA_VOL_RATIO: float = 10.0
MIN_EWMA_VARIANCE: float = 1e-12

# ==============================================================================
# CREDIT RISK CONSTANTS
# ==============================================================================

NO_PARENT_OBLIGOR: np.int64 = np.int64(-1)
NO_RATING_ID: np.int8 = np.int8(-1)
NO_SECTOR_ID: np.int32 = np.int32(-1)
NO_COUNTRY_ID: np.int32 = np.int32(-1)

# Degrees-of-freedom sentinel for non-chi credit factors. nu is strictly
# positive for a chi-square mixing factor, so a negative value is an
# unambiguous "this factor carries no nu" marker (sentinel over NaN, per the
# core blocks contract).
NO_CHI_NU: np.float64 = np.float64(-1.0)

# Beta(alpha, beta) parameter sentinel for non-BETA_PD credit factors. Both
# shape parameters are strictly positive for a Beta factor, so a negative value
# is an unambiguous "this factor carries no alpha/beta" marker.
NO_BETA_PARAM: np.float64 = np.float64(-1.0)

# ==============================================================================
# RANDOM SEED FOR TESTING
# ==============================================================================

RNG_SEED: int = 42

# ==============================================================================
# DAYS PER YEAR FOR TTE CALCULATION
# ==============================================================================

DAYS_PER_YR_ACT_365_25: float = 365.25
TTE_FLOOR: float = 1e-9

# ==============================================================================
# IMPLIED VOL CALIBRATION CONSTANTS
# ==============================================================================

#: Failure sentinel written into the iv output for non-invertible option quotes.
#: Negative by construction, so the downstream contract ``success_mask = iv >= 0.0``
#: flags it as a failed solve. Imported by the Black-76 scalar solver and asserted
#: against by the CalibratedOptionQuotes master.
IV_SOLVER_FAILED_SENTINEL = -1.0

#: Reliability floor for calibrated implied vols, expressed as vega / forward.
#: A solved iv reprices its target even when vega is ~0, but such a vol is
#: under-determined (deep ITM/OTM, ultra-short tenors). Quotes with
#: ``vega < MIN_RELIABLE_VEGA_OVER_FWD * fwd`` are gated out by
#: ``CalibratedOptionQuotes.reliable_mask``. Heuristic; tune per surface.
MIN_RELIABLE_VEGA_OVER_FWD: float = 1e-3
```

```python
# riskquant/exceptions.py


class PrettyKeyError(KeyError):
    """
    KeyError variant that prints the message using str(...) instead of repr(...),
    allowing multi-line, human-readable diagnostics while preserving KeyError
    semantics.
    """

    def __str__(self) -> str:
        return str(self.args[0])


class RiskQuantError(Exception):
    """Base exception for all custom riskquant errors."""

    pass


class ConfigurationError(RiskQuantError):
    """
    Raised when static configuration, ID namespaces, or
    market data mappings violate the engine's strict contracts.
    """

    pass
```

<file path="riskquant\src\riskquant\numba.py">
```python
# src/riskquant/numba.py

"""
Centralized Numba decorator definitions used throughout RiskQuant.

Rationale
---------
RiskQuant standardizes Numba compilation settings to ensure consistent
performance characteristics, numerical behavior, and cache utilization
across the codebase.

This module provides three decorators:

    @njit
        Default choice for most compiled functions.

    @parallel_njit
        For kernels that parallelize outer loops using ``numba.prange``.

    @inline_njit
        For tiny helper functions that should be inlined into callers to
        eliminate function-call overhead.

By centralizing these definitions, compilation settings can be adjusted in
one location without touching hundreds of kernels throughout the system.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from functools import partial

import numba as nb

# Type aliases for Numba-compiled functions — enforce C-contiguous layout for all
# arrays to ensure the compiler emits straight-line SIMD loops with no dynamic
# stride checks. These are used in type signatures for compiled functions below.
_F64_1D = nb.float64[::1]
_F64_2D = nb.float64[:, ::1]
_F64_3D = nb.float64[:, :, ::1]
_F64_4D = nb.float64[:, :, :, ::1]

_F32_1D = nb.float32[::1]
_F32_2D = nb.float32[:, ::1]
_F32_3D = nb.float32[:, :, ::1]
_F32_4D = nb.float32[:, :, :, ::1]

_I64_1D = nb.int64[::1]
_I32_1D = nb.int32[::1]
_I8_1D = nb.int8[::1]

_BOOL_1D = nb.bool_[::1]

#: Default Numba compiler configuration used throughout RiskQuant.
#:
#: Use for the vast majority of compiled functions.
#:
#: Features:
#:     - Explicit fast-math optimizations
#:     - GIL-free execution
#:     - Bounds-check elimination
#:     - On-disk compilation caching
#:     - NumPy-style floating-point error behavior
#:
#: Example:
#:
#:     @njit
#:     def black_scholes(...):
#:         ...
njit = partial(
    nb.njit,
    fastmath={"contract", "reassoc", "nsz"},
    nogil=True,
    boundscheck=False,
    error_model="numpy",
    cache=True,
)


#: Parallel Numba compiler configuration.
#:
#: Use for computational kernels that distribute work across threads using
#: ``numba.prange``.
#:
#: Typical usage:
#:
#:     @parallel_njit
#:     def price_kernel(...):
#:         for i in nb.prange(num_trades):
#:             ...
#:
#: Avoid for:
#:     - Small loops
#:     - Helper functions
#:     - Functions that do not use ``prange``
parallel_njit = partial(
    nb.njit,
    parallel=True,
    fastmath={"contract", "reassoc", "nsz"},
    nogil=True,
    boundscheck=False,
    error_model="numpy",
    cache=True,
)


#: Inline Numba compiler configuration.
#:
#: Intended for extremely small helper functions that are called repeatedly
#: inside hot loops.
#:
#: Examples:
#:     - Normal CDF approximations
#:     - Small mathematical transforms
#:     - Utility functions used inside pricing kernels
#:
#: The ``inline="always"`` hint requests that Numba inline the function body
#: into callers whenever possible.
#:
#: ``error_model="numpy"`` is set to match ``njit``/``parallel_njit``: division
#: by zero yields inf/nan rather than raising inside a nopython (and possibly
#: parallel) caller. Without it, an unguarded division in an inlined helper can
#: turn into a hard ``ZeroDivisionError`` mid-kernel.
inline_njit = partial(
    nb.njit,
    fastmath={"contract", "reassoc", "nsz"},
    nogil=True,
    boundscheck=False,
    error_model="numpy",
    cache=True,
    inline="always",
)
```
</file>

<file path="riskquant\src\riskquant\portfolio\compiled.py">
```python
# src/riskquant/portfolio/compiled.py

"""
Compiled portfolio container.

`CompiledPortfolio` is a pure portfolio-layer artifact: the frozen, valuation-
ready output of block compilation. It holds compiled SoA blocks plus the
metadata the engine needs to walk them — and nothing from the valuation layer.

Layering
--------
This module imports only `portfolio.blocks`. It deliberately does NOT import
pricers, risk evaluators, or the `Evaluator` protocol. Evaluator resolution is
the valuation engine's responsibility: it dispatches on each block's
`INSTRUMENT_ID` class constant via its own `BLOCK_EVALUATORS` table.

The dependency therefore points one way only:

    valuation  ──▶  portfolio        (allowed)
    portfolio  ──▶  valuation        (never)

The previous design bound pricer callables into `_evaluators` inside
`__post_init__`, which made a data container reach up into the valuation layer
and created the `compiled <-> valuation.engine` import cycle. Removing those
references eliminates the cycle by construction — there is no edge left to
break, deferred-import trick or otherwise.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from collections.abc import Iterator
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from typing import TypeAlias, TypedDict

import numpy as np
import numpy.typing as npt

from riskquant.valuation.blocks.spot_block import SpotBlock
from riskquant.valuation.blocks.vanilla_european_option_block import (
    VanillaEuropeanOptionCurveBlock,
    VanillaEuropeanOptionNodeBlock,
)
from riskquant.valuation.blocks.vanilla_forward_block import (
    VanillaForwardCurveBlock,
    VanillaForwardNodeBlock,
)

BlockT: TypeAlias = (
    SpotBlock
    | VanillaForwardNodeBlock
    | VanillaForwardCurveBlock
    | VanillaEuropeanOptionNodeBlock
    | VanillaEuropeanOptionCurveBlock
)


class CompiledPortfolioFields(TypedDict, total=False):
    unique_dates_ord32: npt.NDArray[np.int32]
    spot: SpotBlock | None
    vanilla_forward_node: VanillaForwardNodeBlock | None
    vanilla_forward_curve: VanillaForwardCurveBlock | None
    vanilla_european_option_node: VanillaEuropeanOptionNodeBlock | None
    vanilla_european_option_curve: VanillaEuropeanOptionCurveBlock | None


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True, kw_only=True)
class CompiledPortfolio:
    """
    Valuation-ready portfolio: an ordered set of compiled SoA blocks.

    Each populated block contributes one contiguous row-band to the eventual
    (n_trades x n_scenarios) valuation matrix; `block_offsets[i]` is the first
    row of block `i`. `trade_uids` aggregates every block's uids into one flat
    int64 array in the same order, so a portfolio-row index maps straight to a
    trade uid without per-block bookkeeping.
    """

    # Unique dates across the portfolio, as ordinals truncated to int32 for
    # engine-friendly storage and fast comparisons. Sorted ascending, unique.
    unique_dates_ord32: npt.NDArray[np.int32] = field(
        default_factory=lambda: np.empty(0, dtype=np.int32)
    )

    # Instrument blocks (SoA arrays); None blocks are compacted in __post_init__.
    spot: SpotBlock | None = None
    vanilla_forward_node: VanillaForwardNodeBlock | None = None
    vanilla_forward_curve: VanillaForwardCurveBlock | None = None
    vanilla_european_option_node: VanillaEuropeanOptionNodeBlock | None = None
    vanilla_european_option_curve: VanillaEuropeanOptionCurveBlock | None = None

    # Derived, set in __post_init__.
    _blocks: tuple[BlockT, ...] = field(init=False, repr=False)
    _block_offsets: npt.NDArray[np.int64] = field(init=False, repr=False)
    _total_blocks: int = field(init=False, repr=False)
    _total_trades: int = field(init=False, repr=False)
    _trade_uids: npt.NDArray[np.int64] = field(init=False, repr=False)

    def __post_init__(self) -> None:
        # Construction-ordered slots; None slots are compacted out below. This
        # tuple order defines block ordering and therefore output-matrix bands.
        ordered: tuple[BlockT | None, ...] = (
            self.spot,
            self.vanilla_forward_node,
            self.vanilla_forward_curve,
            self.vanilla_european_option_node,
            self.vanilla_european_option_curve,
        )
        blocks: tuple[BlockT, ...] = tuple(b for b in ordered if b is not None)
        total_blocks: int = len(blocks)

        # Offsets via exclusive prefix-sum of per-block trade counts.
        block_offsets = np.zeros(total_blocks, dtype=np.int64)
        trade_counts = np.fromiter(
            (b.num_trades for b in blocks), dtype=np.int64, count=total_blocks
        )
        total_trades = int(trade_counts.sum())
        if total_blocks > 1:
            np.cumsum(trade_counts[:-1], out=block_offsets[1:])

        # Aggregate every block's uids into one flat array in portfolio order.
        trade_uids = np.empty(total_trades, dtype=np.int64)
        offset = 0
        for block in blocks:
            n = block.num_trades
            trade_uids[offset : offset + n] = block.trade_uids
            offset += n

        object.__setattr__(self, "_blocks", blocks)
        object.__setattr__(self, "_block_offsets", block_offsets)
        object.__setattr__(self, "_total_blocks", total_blocks)
        object.__setattr__(self, "_total_trades", total_trades)
        object.__setattr__(self, "_trade_uids", trade_uids)

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------ #
    # Read-only accessors
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------ #

    @property
    def blocks(self) -> tuple[BlockT, ...]:
        return self._blocks

    @property
    def block_offsets(self) -> npt.NDArray[np.int64]:
        return self._block_offsets

    @property
    def total_blocks(self) -> int:
        return self._total_blocks

    @property
    def total_trades(self) -> int:
        return self._total_trades

    @property
    def trade_uids(self) -> npt.NDArray[np.int64]:
        return self._trade_uids

    def iter_blocks(self) -> Iterator[BlockT]:
        yield from self._blocks

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------ #
    # Diagnostics
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------ #

    def summary(self) -> None:
        """Print a console-friendly overview of block layout and trade counts."""
        print("=" * 90)
        print(f"{'COMPILED PORTFOLIO SUMMARY':^90}")
        print("=" * 90)
        print(f"Total Trades: {self._total_trades:,}")
        print(f"Total Blocks: {self._total_blocks}")
        print(f"Unique Dates: {len(self.unique_dates_ord32)}")
        print("-" * 90)
        print(f"{'Block Type':<32} | {'Trades':<10} | {'Offset'}")
        print("-" * 90)

        if self._total_blocks == 0:
            print(f"{'Empty Portfolio':^90}")
        else:
            for i, block in enumerate(self._blocks):
                print(
                    f"{type(block).__name__:<32} | "
                    f"{block.num_trades:<10,} | {self._block_offsets[i]}"
                )
        print("=" * 90 + "\n")

    def print_block_details(
        self,
        *,
        max_items: int | None = 10,
        float_precision: int = 0,
        sep: str = ", ",
        pad: int = 12,
        thousand_sep: str = "_",
    ) -> None:
        """Cascade into each block and print its raw SoA arrays."""
        print("=" * 90)
        print(f"{f'BLOCK DETAILS (MAX {max_items} ITEMS PER BLOCK)':^90}")
        print("=" * 90 + "\n")
        for block in self._blocks:
            title = f"Block: {type(block).__name__}"
            print("-" * len(title))
            print(title)
            print("-" * len(title))
            block.print_block(
                max_items=max_items,
                float_precision=float_precision,
                sep=sep,
                pad=pad,
                thousand_sep=thousand_sep,
            )
        print("=" * 90 + "\n")
```
</file>

```python
# src/riskquant/portfolio/compilers/instruments/spot.py
"""
Spot block compiler.

Translates the spot-instrument sub-range of an `IngestBatch` (global IDs) into
a `SpotBlock` (execution UIXs) via the run's `UixMapper`. The only fields
that need translation are:
  - `price_uix`            : underlying_ids        -> market_id_universe
  - `fx_spots_effective_uix`: (fx_u2s_ids, fx_u2s_inv) -> fx_effective_pairs

`qty` and `trade_uids` are pure economics — no translation needed; slicing
returns NumPy views (zero copy).
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from typing import TYPE_CHECKING

from riskquant.portfolio.ingest.batch import IngestBatch
from riskquant.valuation.blocks.spot_block import SpotBlock

if TYPE_CHECKING:
    from riskquant.universe import UixMapper


def compile_spot_block(
    batch: IngestBatch,
    start: int,
    end: int,
    uix_mapper: UixMapper,
) -> SpotBlock | None:
    """Slice + UIX-translate a spot run from `batch[start:end]`."""
    if start == end:
        return None

    # Underlying price IDs -> market-universe UIXs.
    price_uix = uix_mapper.map_market_ids_to_uix(batch.underlying_ids[start:end])

    # FX leg (only the under->settle leg matters for spot).
    fx_spots_effective_uix = uix_mapper.map_fx_legs_to_effective_uix(
        raw_fx_ids=batch.fx_u2s_ids[start:end],
        raw_fx_inv=batch.fx_u2s_inv[start:end],
    )

    return SpotBlock(
        trade_uids=batch.uids[start:end],
        qty=batch.qty[start:end],
        effective_spot_price_uix=effective_spot_price_uix,
    )
```

<file path="riskquant\src\riskquant\portfolio\compilers\registry.py">
```python
# src/riskquant/portfolio/compilers/registry.py
"""
Per-instrument block compiler dispatch table.

Each compiler is a `(batch, start, end, uix_mapper) -> Block | None`
callable. The engine's `compile_portfolio_from_ingest_batch` sorts the
batch by `instrument_id`, partitions it into contiguous runs, and resolves
the compiler for each run by direct integer indexing into `COMPILERS`.

The mapper is passed positionally as the fourth argument so the engine
loop stays branch-light and the call sites are uniform.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from collections.abc import Callable
from typing import TYPE_CHECKING, Final, TypeAlias

from riskquant.valuation.blocks._base_block import Block
from riskquant.portfolio.compilers.block_compilers.spot import compile_spot
from riskquant.portfolio.compilers.block_compilers.vanilla_european_option import (
    compile_vanilla_euro_option_curve,
    compile_vanilla_euro_option_node,
)
from riskquant.portfolio.compilers.block_compilers.vanilla_forward import (
    compile_vanilla_forward_curve,
    compile_vanilla_forward_node,
)
from riskquant.portfolio.ingest.batch import IngestBatch
from riskquant.portfolio.instruments import (
    NUM_INSTRUMENTS,
    SPOT_ID,
    VANILLA_EUROPEAN_OPTION_CURVE_ID,
    VANILLA_EUROPEAN_OPTION_NODE_ID,
    VANILLA_FORWARD_CURVE_ID,
    VANILLA_FORWARD_NODE_ID,
)

if TYPE_CHECKING:
    from riskquant.universe import UixMapper


# ==============================================================================
# Block Name aliases
# ==============================================================================


CompilerFn: TypeAlias = Callable[
    [IngestBatch, int, int, "UixMapper"],
    Block | None,
]


# ==============================================================================
# Dispatch tables
# ==============================================================================

_MAX_INSTRUMENT_ID: Final = NUM_INSTRUMENTS

COMPILERS: Final[list[CompilerFn | None]] = [None] * _MAX_INSTRUMENT_ID
FIELD_NAMES: Final[list[str | None]] = [None] * _MAX_INSTRUMENT_ID


def _register(instrument_id: int, field_name: str, fn: CompilerFn) -> None:
    COMPILERS[instrument_id] = fn
    FIELD_NAMES[instrument_id] = field_name


# ==============================================================================
# Registration (executed once at import)
# ==============================================================================

# --- SPOT ---
_register(
    int(SPOT_ID),
    "spot",
    compile_spot,
)

# --- VANILLA FORWARD ---
_register(
    int(VANILLA_FORWARD_NODE_ID),
    "vanilla_forward_node",
    compile_vanilla_forward_node,
)
_register(
    int(VANILLA_FORWARD_CURVE_ID),
    "vanilla_forward_curve",
    compile_vanilla_forward_curve,
)

# --- VANILLA EUROPEAN OPTION ---
_register(
    int(VANILLA_EUROPEAN_OPTION_NODE_ID),
    "vanilla_european_option_node",
    compile_vanilla_euro_option_node,
)
_register(
    int(VANILLA_EUROPEAN_OPTION_CURVE_ID),
    "vanilla_european_option_curve",
    compile_vanilla_euro_option_curve,
)


# Fail loud on missing registrations at import time.
_unregistered = [i for i, fn in enumerate(COMPILERS) if fn is None]
if _unregistered:
    raise RuntimeError(
        f"No compiler registered for instrument IDs: {_unregistered}. "
        f"Register them in compilers.registry."
    )
```
</file>

<file path="riskquant\src\riskquant\portfolio\engine.py">
```python
# src/riskquant/portfolio/engine.py
"""
Top-level portfolio orchestrator and compiler.

The engine sits exactly on the boundary between Global State (sparse
`IngestBatch`es indexed by `static_config` Risk Factor IDs) and Local
Execution State (`CompiledPortfolio` blocks indexed by zero-based
contiguous UIX coordinates aligned with the `MarketDataMatrix`).

Pipeline
--------
1. `build_uix_mapper(ingest_batches)`
      Discovers the global universe touched by the run (market IDs,
      discount-curve IDs, dates) AND deduplicates every distinct
      `(fx_rf_uix, invert)` pair across all FX legs of every trade. The
      result, a `UixMapper`, is the single source of truth for ID->UIX
      translation downstream.

2. (Caller fetches market data using the discovered universes.)

3. `compile_portfolio_from_ingest_batch(ingest_batch, uix_mapper)`
      Translates every global ID and ordinal date in the batch into UIX
      execution coordinates, then partitions into per-instrument compiled
      blocks via the registered compiler table. Returns a frozen
      `CompiledPortfolio`.

Design invariants
-----------------
- `UixMapper` is immutable. Its `_universe` arrays are sorted, unique, and
  validated once at construction; runtime `map_*` calls trust the contract
  and use `np.searchsorted` for O(log N) vectorized lookup.
- `fx_effective_pairs` is built in the same pass as the market universe so
  the FX-effective UIX space is part of the global contract. Pricers will
  index a `(K, S)` `fx_spots_effective` matrix built by the market-data
  builder; `INVALID_UIX` (< 0) signals "no FX conversion required" and is
  the only branch in the pricer hot path.
- Compilers take `(batch, start, end, mapper)`. They are the ONLY place
  global IDs are translated to UIXs; blocks never see global IDs.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from collections.abc import Sequence

import numpy as np
import numpy.typing as npt

from riskquant.constants import NO_FX_RF_REQUIRED
from riskquant.portfolio.compiled import (
    BlockT,
    CompiledPortfolio,
    CompiledPortfolioFields,
)
from riskquant.portfolio.compilers.registry import COMPILERS, FIELD_NAMES
from riskquant.portfolio.ingest.batch import IngestBatch
from riskquant.universe import UixMapper

# `UixMapper` (the run's UIX universe contract type) was relocated to the leaf
# module `riskquant.universe` (item C2) so market_data can import it at runtime
# without a layering inversion. It is re-exported here so existing
# `from riskquant.portfolio.engine import UixMapper` sites keep working; the
# PRODUCER `build_uix_mapper` (below) stays here because it consumes IngestBatch.
__all__ = [
    "UixMapper",
    "build_uix_mapper",
    "compile_portfolio_from_ingest_batch",
]


# ==============================================================================
# INTERNAL HELPERS
# ==============================================================================


def _dedup_fx_effective_pairs(
    *,
    market_id_universe: npt.NDArray[np.int32],
    raw_fx_id_arrays: tuple[npt.NDArray[np.int32], ...],
    raw_fx_inv_arrays: tuple[npt.NDArray[np.int8], ...],
) -> npt.NDArray[np.int32]:
    """
    Build the deduplicated `(fx_uix, invert)` pair table covering every FX
    leg in the run.

    Inputs are the raw `(fx_id, inv)` arrays from every IngestBatch leg
    (under->settle, strike->settle, ...). The function:
      1. Concatenates them into one stream.
      2. Drops sentinel rows (`fx_id == NO_FX_RF_REQUIRED`).
      3. Translates `fx_id` -> `market_id_universe` UIX.
      4. `np.unique(axis=0)` to get the sorted-unique pair table.

    Returned shape is `(K, 2) int32`. The two columns are
    `(fx_uix_in_market_universe, invert_flag)`. The table is sorted
    lexicographically by `(fx_uix, invert)`, which is critical for the
    `np.searchsorted` lookup that `map_fx_legs_to_effective_uix` performs
    via the packed `key = (uix << 1) | inv` trick.
    """
    if len(raw_fx_id_arrays) != len(raw_fx_inv_arrays):
        raise ValueError(
            f"FX leg array count mismatch: ids={len(raw_fx_id_arrays)}, "
            f"inv={len(raw_fx_inv_arrays)}"
        )

    if not raw_fx_id_arrays:
        return np.empty((0, 2), dtype=np.int32)

    all_ids = np.concatenate(raw_fx_id_arrays)
    all_inv = np.concatenate(raw_fx_inv_arrays)

    mask = all_ids != int(NO_FX_RF_REQUIRED)
    if not mask.any():
        return np.empty((0, 2), dtype=np.int32)

    real_ids = all_ids[mask]
    real_inv = all_inv[mask]

    # Translate raw IDs -> market-universe UIXs.
    real_uix = np.searchsorted(market_id_universe, real_ids).astype(
        np.int32, copy=False
    )

    pairs = np.stack(
        (real_uix, real_inv.astype(np.int32, copy=False)),
        axis=1,
    )
    unique_pairs = np.unique(pairs, axis=0)
    # np.unique already returns sorted lex order; ensure C-contiguous int32.
    return np.ascontiguousarray(unique_pairs.astype(np.int32, copy=False))


# ==============================================================================
# ORCHESTRATOR PHASE (UNIVERSE DISCOVERY)
# ==============================================================================


def build_uix_mapper(ingest_batches: Sequence[IngestBatch]) -> UixMapper:
    """
    Global universe discovery — the "orchestrator pause".

    Walks every `IngestBatch` once to collect:
      - the union of every market ID (underlyings, vols, FX spots)
      - the union of every discount curve ID
      - the union of every trade-relevant date ordinal
      - the deduplicated `(fx_uix, invert)` pair table

    Returns a frozen `UixMapper` that downstream consumers (block compilers,
    market-data builder, validation) use as the single source of truth for
    UIX <-> global-ID translation.
    """
    if not ingest_batches:
        raise ValueError(
            "No ingest batches provided to build_uix_mapper. At least one "
            "IngestBatch is required."
        )

    # ---- MARKET IDs ----
    market_id_arrays = [
        arr
        for b in ingest_batches
        for arr in (b.underlying_ids, b.volatility_ids, b.fx_u2s_ids, b.fx_k2s_ids)
    ]
    raw_market = np.concatenate(market_id_arrays)
    market_id_universe = np.unique(raw_market[raw_market >= 0]).astype(
        np.int32, copy=False
    )

    # ---- DISCOUNT CURVE IDs ----
    discount_id_arrays = [
        arr
        for b in ingest_batches
        for arr in (b.under_discount_curve_ids, b.strike_discount_curve_ids)
    ]
    raw_discount = np.concatenate(discount_id_arrays)
    discount_curve_id_universe = np.unique(raw_discount[raw_discount >= 0]).astype(
        np.int32, copy=False
    )

    # ---- DATES ----
    date_arrays = [
        arr
        for b in ingest_batches
        for arr in (b.settle_ord, b.delivery_ord, b.expiry_ord)
    ]
    raw_dates = np.concatenate(date_arrays)
    date_ord32_universe = np.unique(raw_dates[raw_dates >= 0]).astype(
        np.int32, copy=False
    )

    # ---- FX EFFECTIVE PAIRS ----
    raw_fx_id_arrays = tuple(
        arr for b in ingest_batches for arr in (b.fx_u2s_ids, b.fx_k2s_ids)
    )
    raw_fx_inv_arrays = tuple(
        arr for b in ingest_batches for arr in (b.fx_u2s_inv, b.fx_k2s_inv)
    )
    fx_effective_pairs = _dedup_fx_effective_pairs(
        market_id_universe=market_id_universe,
        raw_fx_id_arrays=raw_fx_id_arrays,
        raw_fx_inv_arrays=raw_fx_inv_arrays,
    )

    return UixMapper(
        market_id_universe=market_id_universe,
        discount_curve_id_universe=discount_curve_id_universe,
        date_ord32_universe=date_ord32_universe,
        fx_effective_pairs=fx_effective_pairs,
    )


# ==============================================================================
# COMPILER PHASE (UIX INJECTION)
# ==============================================================================


def compile_portfolio_from_ingest_batch(
    *,
    ingest_batch: IngestBatch,
    uix_mapper: UixMapper,
) -> CompiledPortfolio:
    """
    Sort an `IngestBatch` by `instrument_id` and compile each contiguous run
    into an execution-ready block using the provided `UixMapper`.

    The function performs the only global-ID -> UIX translation in the
    portfolio layer: it hands each instrument-specific compiler the
    sub-batch and the mapper, and the compiler does the actual gather.
    """
    instrument_ids = ingest_batch.instrument_ids
    n = instrument_ids.size

    if n == 0:
        raise ValueError("Cannot compile an empty batch into a portfolio.")

    # ---- 1. Sort once by instrument_id (stable so original ordering wins
    #         within each instrument group). ----
    order = np.argsort(instrument_ids, kind="stable")
    ingest_batch = ingest_batch.reorder(order)
    sorted_ids = ingest_batch.instrument_ids

    # ---- 2. Find group boundaries via one diff + one concat. ----
    change_idx = np.flatnonzero(sorted_ids[1:] != sorted_ids[:-1]) + 1
    boundaries = np.concatenate(([0], change_idx, [n]))

    # ---- 3. Compile per-instrument blocks. ----
    compiled_fields: CompiledPortfolioFields = {}
    compilers = COMPILERS
    field_names = FIELD_NAMES

    n_groups = len(boundaries) - 1
    for i in range(n_groups):
        start = int(boundaries[i])
        end = int(boundaries[i + 1])
        instrument_id = int(sorted_ids[start])

        compiler = compilers[instrument_id]
        if compiler is None:
            continue

        field_name = field_names[instrument_id]
        if field_name is None:
            raise RuntimeError(
                f"Compiler registered for instrument_id={instrument_id} but "
                "no FIELD_NAME mapping. Wire it in compilers.registry."
            )

        block: BlockT | None = compiler(ingest_batch, start, end, uix_mapper)
        if block is not None:
            compiled_fields[field_name] = block

    # ---- 4. Forward portfolio-level metadata to the compiled artifact. ----
    compiled_fields["unique_dates_ord32"] = uix_mapper.date_ord32_universe

    return CompiledPortfolio(**compiled_fields)
```

```python
# riskquant/portfolio/fx.py
from __future__ import annotations

import re
from typing import Iterable

_ccy_re = re.compile(r"^[A-Z]{3}$")


def validate_ccy(ccy: str) -> str:
    if not isinstance(ccy, str):
        raise TypeError(f"Currency must be str; got {type(ccy).__name__}")
    if len(ccy) != 3:
        raise ValueError(
            f"Currency must be exactly 3 letters; got '{ccy}' ({len(ccy)})"
        )
    if _ccy_re.match(ccy) is None:
        raise ValueError(f"Currency must be 3 uppercase letters A–Z; got '{ccy}'")
    return ccy


def validate_ccys_unique(ccys: Iterable[str]) -> None:
    """Validate each distinct CCY once; fail fast on first invalid."""
    for c in set(ccys):
        validate_ccy(c)


def get_fx_pair_for_under_to_settle_conversion(
    *,
    underlying_ccy: str,
    settlement_ccy: str,
) -> str:
    # Validate scalars only (no allocations)
    u = validate_ccy(underlying_ccy)
    s = validate_ccy(settlement_ccy)
    if u == s:
        return ""
    return f"{u}{s}"
```
